Our framework can enable researchers to better assess the distributional breadth of LLM-authored content, which we term its "cultural reach".

</details>


### [136] [SkillZip: Contract-Preserving Graph Compression for Scalable Agent Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05604)
*Xingyu Tan,Xiaoyang Wang,Qing Liu,Xiwei Xu,Xin Yuan,Liming Zhu,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillZip是一个执行感知的程序化抽象框架，通过在section级图上进行契约保留的压缩（将递归的契约有效动机重写为可逆端口宏），解决LLM智能体技能库在有限上下文预算下的最小充分可执行上下文的暴露问题。在技术型和具身智能体基准上，SkillZip以3.46倍压缩比、99.2%依赖保留率和98.7%验证器可达性，超越最强基线最多12.2个百分点，并在200到10万技能库规模下保持稳健检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统在技能库增长时面临核心挑战：无法在技能子级别复用例程、压缩时无法保留程序契约、压缩后的例程难以保持可执行性和可扩展性，以及技能演化时难以更新压缩库。根本问题是单元不匹配——技能作为包检索、作为文本压缩、检索后才转换为执行图，而可靠复用需要携带契约的程序化单元。

Method: SkillZip在section级图上执行契约保留压缩：将递归出现的契约有效动机（motifs）重写为可逆端口宏（portable macros），同时保留边界签名、依赖闭包、验证器可达性和源码级扩展。推理时，SkillZip水合（hydrate）一个紧凑且依赖闭合的上下文，仅在需要时扩展宏。ReZip组件利用执行证据整合新技能并修正有风险的宏。

Result: 在技术型和具身智能体基准上，SkillZip持续超越最强基线最多12.2个百分点，实现3.46倍压缩比、99.2%依赖保留率和98.7%验证器可达性。扩展性分析确认在200到10万技能库规模下检索依旧稳健。

Conclusion: SkillZip通过引入契约承载的程序化单元解决了技能检索与压缩之间的粒度不匹配问题，在压缩率、依赖保留和可执行性之间取得了更好平衡，并能通过ReZip随技能演化进行增量更新，为大规模LLM智能体技能库管理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly act as agents whose procedural knowledge is stored in reusable skill packages and loaded at inference time. As skill libraries grow, a central challenge is to expose the smallest sufficient executable context under a limited context budget. Existing systems struggle to reuse routines below the whole-skill level, preserve procedural contracts during compression, keep compressed routines executable and expandable, and update the compressed library as skills evolve. These challenges reveal a unit mismatch: skills are retrieved as packages, compressed as text, and converted into execution graphs only after retrieval, whereas reliable reuse requires a contract-bearing procedural unit. We propose SkillZip, an execution-aware procedural abstraction framework that performs contract-preserving compression over section-level graphs. SkillZip rewrites recurring contract-valid motifs into reversible ported macros while preserving boundary signatures, dependency closure, verifier reachability, and source-level expansion. At inference time, it hydrates a compact, dependency-closed context and expands macros only when required. ReZip further integrates new skills and revises risky macros using execution evidence. Comprehensive experiments1 on technical and embodied agent benchmarks show SkillZip consistently outperforms the strongest baseline by up to 12.2 points, while achieving a 3.46x compression ratio with 99.2% dependency preservation and 98.7% verifier reachability. Scaling analyses further confirm robust retrieval across skill libraries ranging from 200 to 100K skills.

</details>


### [137] [FOCUS: Decoupling Expert Personas in LLMs to Enhance Domain Expert Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05611)
*Guanyu Wang,Zidi Zhang,Xu Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FOCUS提出了一种通过正交分解解耦领域专属专家人格向量，并结合专家门控模块自适应控制人格激活的方法，在金融、法律、医疗及跨领域基准上提升了任务准确率，优于现有的人格控制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM人格控制方法存在跨领域耦合问题：在高谨慎领域（如医疗）可能表现过于激进，在风险敏感领域（如金融交易）可能过度保守。需要一种去耦合的人格激活机制来消除这些负面影响。

Method: FOCUS包含三个关键步骤：（1）自动从LLM中提取专家人格向量；（2）通过正交分解解耦领域特定的专家人格；（3）引入专家门控模块，根据任务上下文自适应控制人格激活。采用两阶段训练策略和门控选择正则化器，使模型学会在单域和跨域任务中激活合适的人格。

Result: 在金融、法律、医疗和跨领域基准测试上的实验表明，FOCUS提升了任务准确率，并优于现有的其他人格控制方法。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过正交化人格解耦和自适应门控机制，有效解决了LLM人格控制中的跨领域耦合问题，能够在多领域任务中更精准地激活合适的专家人格。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can exhibit diverse personas, and activating expert personas has been shown to improve domain expertise and task accuracy. However, existing persona control methods often suffer from cross-domain coupling, which may lead to overly aggressive behavior in high-caution domains such as healthcare, or excessive conservatism in risk-sensitive domains such as financial trading. To address this issue, we propose FOCUS (\textbf{\underline{F}}ine-tuning with \textbf{\underline{O}}rthogonal \textbf{\underline{C}}ontrol for \textbf{\underline{U}}ncoupled persona\textbf{\underline{S}}). FOCUS first automatically extracts expert persona vectors from LLMs, then applies orthogonal decomposition to decouple domain-specific expert personas, and finally introduces an expert gating module to adaptively control persona activation according to task contexts. With a two-stage training strategy and a gated selection regularizer, the model learns to activate appropriate personas for both single-domain and cross-domain tasks. Experiments on financial, legal, medical, and cross-domain benchmarks show that FOCUS improves task accuracy and outperforms existing persona control methods. Our code is available at \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/openpersona-48F4}{this url}.

</details>


### [138] [Human-Like Anaphor Resolution in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05630)
*Keane Zhang,Varshini Chinta,Raj Sanjay Shah,Sashank Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种开源大语言模型（GPT-2-XL、Llama-3.1-8B、Pythia-12B、Mistral-7B和Mistral-24B）在回指消解中对认知科学所识别的多种因素（语篇结构、情境模型属性和语义因素）的敏感性。通过将模型困惑度与人类阅读时间相关联，并比较模型与人类在理解问题上的准确率，发现这些模型表现出选择性认知对齐：部分LLM对语篇突显性和距离因素具有类人敏感性，但对语义干扰效应的敏感性较弱或缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回指消解是理解自然语言的核心过程之一。认知科学已发现语篇结构、情境模型属性和语义因素等多种因素影响人类回指消解的速度和成功率。该研究旨在探究这些认知因素是否同样影响大语言模型的回指消解行为，以评估LLM在多大程度上近似人类的语言处理机制。

Method: 研究采用两种行为测量手段：(1) 采用标准连接假说，将人类阅读时间与模型在回指词处的困惑度（surprisal）相关联；(2) 比较模型与人类在探测回指先行词的理解问题上的准确率。研究对象包括五种开放权重的大语言模型：GPT-2-XL、Llama-3.1-8B、Pythia-12B、Mistral-7B和Mistral-24B。

Result: 结果表明存在选择性认知对齐现象：部分LLM在回指消解中对语篇突显性和距离相关因素表现出类人敏感性，但对语义干扰效应的敏感性较弱或完全缺失。不同模型在认知对齐程度上存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究界定并厘清了LLM在何种条件下能够近似人类回指消解行为，发现LLM对人类相关认知因素的敏感度是有选择性的而非全面的，这对于理解LLM作为人类语言处理计算模型的有效性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Anaphors are expressions that refer to other expressions, called antecedents. The process of connecting the two is called resolution. Cognitive science has identified multiple factors that affect the speed and success of anaphor resolution, including discourse structure, situation-model properties, and semantic factors. Here, we investigate whether these factors also affect anaphor resolution in five Large Language Models (LLMs) with open weights: GPT-2-XL, Llama-3.1-8B, Pythia-12B, Mistral-7B, and Mistral-24B. To model processing difficulty, we adopt the standard linking hypothesis that relates human reading times to model surprisal at the anaphor. As a second behavioral measure, we compare model accuracy to human accuracy on comprehension questions probing the antecedents of anaphors. The results show selective cognitive alignment: some LLMs exhibit human-like sensitivity to discourse prominence and distance-based factors in anaphor resolution, while showing weaker or absent sensitivity to semantic interference effects. These findings delimit the conditions under which LLMs approximate human anaphor resolution.

</details>


### [139] [Relay, Don't Route: Adaptive Population Handoff for Cost-Efficient LLM-Driven Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05651)
*Sichun Luo,Yi Huang,Guanzhi Deng,Haibo Wang,Haochen Luo,Lei Li,Zefa Hu,Junlan Feng,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无训练框架EvoPress，通过自适应种群交接（population handoff）在固定推理预算下智能分配廉价与强大语言模型，实现高效的程序搜索与算法发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的进化搜索虽有效但依赖强模型成本高昂，现有方法只在单次查询或变异步骤层面分配模型，忽略了进化搜索具有状态性（stateful）——每个候选生成都会改变后续变异的种群。已有研究显示搜索进度高度前载，早期轨迹信息有价值但有噪声，廉价模型能以更低成本恢复强模型的大部分早期进展，因此需要一种面向种群而非单次调用的预算分配策略。

Method: 提出无训练框架：廉价模型在多条轨迹上以短块方式探索，由bandit调度器分配资源；定义Relay Gain（交接候选库的边际改进）作为调度奖励来决定何时交接；通过质量多样性（quality-diverse）筛选的紧凑候选库初始化共享强模型种群进行精炼。

Result: 在四个基准和三种预算下，模型在12个设置中的11个取得最高平均分，优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: 在有状态搜索中，预算分配应围绕种群而非单次调用进行组织，这为LLM驱动的进化搜索提供了更高效的成本收益权衡方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-driven evolution has shown promise for program search and algorithm discovery, but relying on strong models throughout long evolutionary runs is costly. A natural alternative is to combine cheap and strong models under a fixed inference budget. However, existing approaches typically allocate models at the level of individual queries or mutation steps, overlooking that evolutionary search is \textit{stateful}: each generated candidate changes the population from which subsequent mutations are produced.
  We empirically analyze LLM-driven evolutionary trajectories and find that search progress is strongly front-loaded, early trajectory performance is informative but noisy, and cheap models recover much of the early progress achieved by strong models at lower cost. Motivated by these findings, we propose \textbf{\model}, a training-free framework that shifts budget allocation from individual calls to evolving populations through adaptive \textit{population handoff}. A cheap model explores multiple trajectories in short blocks allocated by a bandit scheduler. Relay Gain, defined as the marginal improvement of a compact, quality-diverse candidate bank constructed for handoff, serves as the scheduler reward and determines when to hand off. The curated candidates initialize a shared strong model population for refinement. Across four benchmarks and three budgets, \model achieves the highest mean score in 11 of 12 settings, outperforming competitive baselines. Our results suggest that in stateful search, budget allocation should be organized around the population, not the individual call.

</details>


### [140] [Answer First, Reason Later: Commitment Order in Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05687)
*Jewon Yeom,Jaewon Sok,Seonghyeon Park,Jeongjae Park,Hwiyeong Lee,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了掩码扩散语言模型（dLLM）在推理任务上的核心缺陷：其任意顺序提交token的自由度反而导致过早承诺最终答案、忽略推理链，进而引发推理能力崩溃。作者提出"前沿门控提交"（frontier-gated commitment）单一干预手段，可因果性地恢复全部推理差距（0.528→0.852），同时保留最高4倍并行解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM（如LLaDA）号称可任意顺序提交token，这是其相对于自回归解码的核心卖点。但作者发现此自由度在推理任务中恰是失败根源——纯解码在路径仅完成15-24%时就提交最终答案，且画布增大时高达90%的问题退化为仅输出答案。此前的窗口式采样器（window-style samplers）仅被视为效率工具，作者希望重新定义其为解决推理病理的必需机制。

Method: 作者在LLaDA-8B上对GSM8K解码过程逐token记录提交日志；通过2x2提示词×解码器设计（有/无思维链 × 有序/无序提交）分解出"崩溃通道"与"顺序通道"两种失效机制；并在Dream-7B和MATH-500上复现验证。提出"前沿门控提交"干预：通过测量得到的"前沿窗口"（frontier window）控制每步可提交的token范围，其最优窗口从全精炼时的w=1翻转为8 token/步时的无约束。

Result: 1) 纯解码在推理路径15-24%处就提交最终答案，超50%推理区域仍被掩码；2) 画布增大时高达90%问题退化为答案-only；3) 终止信念（EOS压力）在不同解码器间几乎一致，失败源于可达性而非信念；4) 思维链仅在有序提交下有效（交互效应+34.8个百分点，95% CI [26.8, 42.8]）；5) 前沿门控提交将准确率从0.528提升至0.852，同时保留4倍并行加速。两个失效通道独立识别并跨模型跨基准复现。

Conclusion: dLLM的并行解码自由度在推理场景下表现为病理性的过早承诺。此前被视作效率折衷的窗口采样器，其真正价值在于修复推理缺陷——一个它们从未被设计来应对的问题。作者提出的前沿门控机制表明只需单一旋钮即可同时兼顾推理质量与并行效率，为dLLM推理能力设计与现有采样器提供了统一解释框架。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (dLLMs) can commit tokens in any order -- a freedom marketed as their core advantage over autoregressive decoding. We show that on reasoning tasks this freedom is instead the axis of failure. Logging every commitment during decoding of LLaDA-8B on GSM8K, we find that unconstrained (pure) decoding commits the final answer at 15-24% of the trajectory while half the reasoning region is still masked, and collapses to answer-only outputs on up to 90% of problems as the canvas grows. The cause is not the model's termination beliefs -- EOS "pressure" is nearly identical across decoders -- but reachability: whether the sampler may act on those beliefs at distant positions. A 2x2 prompt-decoder design shows that chain-of-thought helps only under ordered commitment (interaction +34.8 percentage points, 95% CI [26.8, 42.8]; without reasoning text the decoders are indistinguishable), an interaction we decompose into a collapse channel and an order channel and replicate on Dream-7B and MATH-500. A single-knob intervention -- frontier-gated commitment -- causally recovers the full gap (0.528 to 0.852) while preserving up to 4x parallel decoding, along a measured frontier whose optimal window flips from w=1 at full refinement to unconstrained at 8 tokens/step. Our results reframe existing window-style samplers, previously motivated by efficiency, as the minimal fix for a reasoning pathology they were never designed to address.

</details>


### [141] [Sparse Mutual Information Graph Averaging for Improving Random Indexing Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05724)
*Sriram Loganathan,Gokul Anand,Aung Bo Bo,Yourui Shao,William B. Andreopoulos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在稀疏PPMI图上通过加权平均进行精化的随机索引（RI）向量。在童话语料库上，PPMI top-K图平均将RI的类比准确率从19.4%提升至30.7%，但对PPMI+SVD、Binary+SVD、CBOW和Skip-gram等基线产生负面影响，且在text8和SimLex-999上表现不佳，仅作为弱RI嵌入的有效非梯度修复手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否仅依赖稀疏全局语料统计（避免稠密共现矩阵物化、稠密分解和梯度训练）来构建词嵌入，并研究如何修复弱随机索引（RI）初始化的嵌入质量。

Method: 使用随机索引（RI）生成稀疏词向量，然后在稀疏PPMI图中通过top-K剪枝和加权平均对RI向量进行精化，并与PPMI+SVD、Binary+SVD、CBOW、Skip-gram等方法在类比任务和相似性相关性上进行对比评估。

Result: 在童话语料库272个Google家族类比问题上，PPMI top-K图平均将RI准确率从19.4±0.7%提升至30.7±2.9%（seed42时最优达34.6%）；但该邻域平均降低了PPMI+SVD等基线方法的表现，在text8上不敌神经基线，且在SimLex-999上严格相似度相关性接近零。

Conclusion: PPMI top-K剪枝图平均是一种有效的非梯度方法，能够显著修复弱RI嵌入，但其整体性能无法与神经基线竞争，适用范围有限。

Abstract: Sparse word embedding pipelines can avoid dense co-occurrence matrix materialization, dense factorization, and gradient training while still relying on sparse global corpus statistics. This paper studies Random Indexing (RI) vectors refined by weighted averaging on a sparse Positive Pointwise Mutual Information (PPMI) graph. On a fairytales corpus, the covered semantic analogy set consists of 272 Google family- category questions. On this family subset, PPMI top-K graph averaging repairs a weak RI initialization, improving accuracy from 19.4+-0.7% to 30.7+-2.9% across five seeds. Under the single tested runs, the same neighborhood averaging reduces family- subset analogy accuracy for PPMI+SVD (singular value decom- position), Binary+SVD, CBOW, and Skip-gram. Thus the method is not competitive with neural baselines on text8 and gives near- zero strict similarity correlation on SimLex-999. While Bloom filter sketches underperform RI in the tested configuration, we find that PPMI graph averaging with top-K pruning is a useful non-gradient repair for weak RI embeddings. On the fairytales dataset, PPMI top-K=50 graph averaging improves RI with accuracy going from 19.4+-0.7% to 30.7+-2.9%, and performing best with a seed42 of 34.6%.

</details>


### [142] [Mitigating Scoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge via Random Number Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05726)
*Yuma Asato,Kiyoaki Shirai,Natthawut Kertkeidkachorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的方法以缓解LLM作为评估者（LLM-as-a-Judge）时的评分偏差（scoring bias）。该方法通过让LLM随机生成数字token来测量其潜在的数字偏差，并根据该偏差对下游任务评估中的token生成概率进行校正。在四个任务（LLM对齐评估、摘要评估、语义文本相似度和语义文本相关性）上的实验表明，该方法优于无去偏的LLM和现有校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常被用作文本质量评估器（LLM-as-a-Judge），其性能优于依赖参考文本的传统自动评估指标。然而，LLM评估器倾向于生成特定分数而不考虑被评估文本的上下文，这被称为评分偏差（scoring bias），严重影响了评估的可靠性和公正性。

Method: 首先，指令LLM随机生成数字token，通过观测数字分布与均匀分布的偏差来识别LLM的潜在数值偏差。其次，在随机数生成的提示中加入下游任务的定义，以测量任务特定的潜在数字偏差。最后，在LLM实际评估时，根据测得的潜在数字偏差对输入给定下token的生成概率进行修正（rectification）。

Result: 在四个不同任务（LLM对齐评估、摘要评估、语义文本相似度STS、语义文本相关性STR）上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法优于基线方法（包括未去偏的LLM和先前的校准方法）。此外，实验证实评分偏差因LLM模型、任务类型和评分范围的不同而有所差异，表明按需测量潜在数字偏差的重要性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于潜在数字偏差测量的去偏方法能有效缓解LLM评估器的评分偏差，并且由于偏差具有模型、任务和评分范围的差异性，动态测量潜在数字偏差对于构建可靠的LLM评估器至关重要。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are often used as evaluators of text quality, known as LLM-as-a-Judge, which can outperform conventional automatic evaluation metrics that rely on reference texts. However, LLM evaluators tend to generate particular scores regardless of the context of the evaluated text, which is known as scoring bias. This study proposes a novel method to mitigate this scoring bias. An LLM is instructed to randomly generate number tokens, and the latent numerical bias of the LLM is identified by measuring the deviation of the observed distribution of numbers from the uniform distribution. A definition of a downstream task, for which an LLM evaluator is used, is added to the prompts for random number generation to measure task-specific latent number bias. In the evaluation by an LLM, the token generation probabilities for a given input are rectified considering the LLM's latent number bias. Results of the experiment on four different tasks, evaluation of LLM alignment, evaluation of summarization, Semantic Textual Similarity, and Semantic Textual Relatedness, demonstrate that our proposed method outperforms the baselines, including an LLM without debiasing and previous calibration methods. In addition, it is confirmed that scoring bias varies across LLMs, tasks, and score ranges, indicating the importance of measuring latent number bias as the case may be.

</details>


### [143] [Once a Response, Always a Response: Detecting LLM-generated Text via Latent Prompt Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05741)
*Hongrui Bao,Yubing Ren,Yanan Cao,Jinhan You,Fang Fang,Shi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EchoPrompt是一种基于隐式提示恢复的训练无关（zero-shot）机器生成文本检测器，通过前置统一通用前缀来部分重新激活机器文本的上游提示依赖，并利用指令微调模型与基座模型之间的似然增益差异进行评分，在多个评测设定下达到最优性能且保持强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的流畅文本带来了虚假信息传播、教育滥用和平台治理等风险，亟需鲁棒的机器文本检测方法。现有零样本检测器主要利用基于概率的统计差异，但未显式建模LLM的训练过程，导致生成机制建模不充分、检测鲁棒性受限。

Method: EchoPrompt基于隐式提示恢复的核心思想：机器生成文本通常是在上游提示条件下生成的，这种隐藏依赖可通过前置统一通用前缀部分重新激活。具体实现为：(1) 恢复通用助手指令上下文；(2) 用指令微调模型测量由此引起的似然增益；(3) 与对应基座模型进行校准；(4) 聚合差异得到量化隐式提示依赖的检测分数。整个过程无需训练。

Result: 大量实验表明，EchoPrompt在零样本检测器中达到最先进的性能，并在多种具有挑战性的评测设定下保持强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式利用LLM训练过程中形成的提示-生成依赖关系，EchoPrompt为机器生成文本检测提供了一种新的、更鲁棒的零样本检测范式，在保持训练无关的同时提升了检测性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate fluent and convincing text at scale, creating growing risks for misinformation dissemination, educational misuse, and platform governance. These concerns make robust detection of machine-generated text increasingly necessary. Recent zero-shot detectors mainly exploit probability-based statistical discrepancies, but they do not explicitly account for the training process of LLMs, which leaves a distinct generation mechanism insufficiently modeled and limits detection robustness. To address this issue, we propose EchoPrompt, a training-free detector based on latent prompt restoration. Our key intuition is that machine-generated text is typically produced conditioned on an upstream prompt, and this hidden dependency can be partially reactivated by prepending a unified generic prefix. Specifically, EchoPrompt restores a generic assistant-response context, measures the induced likelihood gain with an instruction-tuned model, calibrates it against the corresponding base model, and aggregates the resulting differences into a score that quantifies latent prompt dependency. Extensive experiments show that EchoPrompt achieves state-of-the-art performance among zero-shot detectors while maintaining strong robustness across challenging evaluation settings.

</details>


### [144] [How to Recognize New Words: A Comparison Between Context Biasing Methods and Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05759)
*Christian Huber,Alexander Waibel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种处理ASR中稀有/新词（命名实体、缩略词、领域专有词）的策略：基于Whisper的上下文偏置（context biasing）方法和基于语音大语言模型（speech LLM）的上下文提示方法，评估了两者在朗读和非朗读语音上的词错误率表现。上下文偏置方法可将偏置词错误率相对降低最多88%且不影响其他词；语音LLM在朗读语音上表现优异但对非朗读语音泛化较差，且对干扰词数量与提示词顺序敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动语音识别（ASR）在处理训练数据中稀缺的新词和罕见词（如命名实体、缩略词、领域专有词）时仍面临重大挑战。现有方法如上下文偏置和语音大语言模型各有优劣，但缺乏系统性的定量比较，研究者希望明确两种策略的适用场景与权衡。

Method: 作者对比了两类方法：基于Whisper的两种上下文偏置方法（在推理时扩展模型以接受词表输入）以及三种语音大语言模型（通过直接提示上下文）。在朗读和非朗读语音上分别评估，报告偏置词错误率（biased WER）、非偏置词错误率（unbiased WER）以及总体词错误率（overall WER）。

Result: 上下文偏置方法可将偏置词错误率相对降低最多88%，且对非偏置词影响很小。语音LLM在朗读语音上表现优异，但对非朗读语音泛化能力较弱，同时对干扰词数量（distractor count）和提示词顺序表现出较高的敏感性。

Conclusion: 两种策略各有适用场景：上下文偏置方法更适合对非偏置词影响小、稳定提升偏置词识别率的场景；语音LLM适合朗读语音场景但有泛化局限和提示敏感性。作者总结了这些权衡以指导方法选择。

Abstract: Recognizing new and rare words - named entities, acronyms, domain specific special words, and other items scarce in training data - remains a key challenge for automatic speech recognition (ASR). We compare two strategies for this: context biasing methods, where an ASR model is extended such that during inference a word list can be supplied, and speech large language models (LLMs) prompted with context directly. We evaluate two context biasing methods based on Whisper against three speech LLMs across read and non-read speech, reporting biased, unbiased, and overall word error rate (WER). The context biasing methods cut biased WER by up to 88% relative while leaving other words largely unaffected. Speech LLMs excel on read speech but generalize less well to non-read speech, and prove sensitive to distractor count and prompt word order. We characterize the resulting trade-offs to guide method selection.

</details>


### [145] [Task-Conditional Flow Matching for Balanced Multilingual Text Embedding Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05785)
*Tirth Bhatt,Naren Kumar S,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务条件流匹配框架（TCFM），将流匹配选择性应用于翻译任务，同时对检索、分类和配对分类任务使用更匹配其学习动态的目标函数，并辅以教师引导的表示保持和三阶段课程学习，在印地语大规模文本嵌入基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言文本嵌入模型通常使用单一训练目标跨不同任务进行适配，但不同任务本质需要不同的优化策略，单一目标无法满足各类任务的异构学习需求。

Method: TCFM框架的核心包括：（1）任务条件化设计，即仅对翻译任务应用流匹配目标，对检索、分类和配对分类任务则使用与其学习动态更匹配的优化目标；（2）教师引导的表示保持机制，稳定适配过程；（3）三阶段课程学习策略，实现渐进式且稳定的训练。

Result: 在Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark上，TCFM取得了新的最先进结果，在多语言任务集合上一致提升嵌入质量，且可泛化到不同的嵌入模型家族。

Conclusion: 任务条件化的目标选择策略优于统一训练目标，能够显著提升多语言文本嵌入在异构任务上的表现；论文公开代码库和数据集以促进研究进展。

Abstract: Multilingual text embedding models are commonly adapted using a single training objective across diverse tasks, despite different tasks requiring fundamentally different optimization strategies. We introduce Task-Conditional Flow Matching (TCFM), a multilingual embedding adaptation framework that selectively applies Flow Matching to translation tasks while optimizing retrieval, classification, and pair-classification tasks with objectives better aligned to their learning dynamics. TCFM further combines teacher-guided representation preservation with a three-stage curriculum to enable stable adaptation. Evaluated on the Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, TCFM establishes a new state-of-the-art, consistently improving embedding quality across a diverse set of multilingual tasks and generalizing across embedding model families. We will publicly release the codebase and datasets upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [146] [On-Policy Delta Distillation for Multilingual Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05802)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）及其变体OPD²在多语言（英语、韩语、日语）数学推理中的应用，发现OPD²一致优于原始OPD，尤其在韩语和日语上提升显著，且能缩小英语-韩语性能差距；同时发现仅用英语数据的OPD虽能提升其他语言性能，但会导致回答偏向英语，强调多语言数据的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为LLM后训练中强化学习的替代方案正在兴起，但其在多语言场景下的有效性尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，研究OPD在非英语语言数学推理任务中的表现。

Method: 对比研究OPD与OPD²两种在线策略蒸馏方法，在多语言数学推理任务（英语、韩语、日语）上进行实验。OPD²利用经过后训练的教师模型与其基础模型之间的概率差作为学习信号，以此改进原始的OPD。实验基于Qwen3模型。

Result: 实验表明：OPD²一致优于原始OPD，尤其在韩语和日语上提升明显；OPD²总体上缩小了英语与韩语之间的性能差距；仅使用英语数据的OPD也能提升韩语和日语的性能，但会导致模型响应逐渐偏向英语，失去目标语言特性。

Conclusion: OPD²是一种在多语言数学推理场景下更优的蒸馏方法，多语言训练数据对于保持目标语言的响应方式至关重要；仅依靠英语数据进行蒸馏虽然能提升性能，但会牺牲语言保真度。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is emerging as a promising alternative to reinforcement learning for LLM post-training, yet its effectiveness in multilingual settings remains underexplored. We study OPD and its advanced variant, On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), for mathematical reasoning in English, Korean, and Japanese. OPD$^2$ improves OPD by using the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model as the learning signal. Experiments with Qwen3 show that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms the original OPD, with particularly strong improvements in Korean and Japanese, and generally narrows the English-Korean performance gap. We further find that English-only OPD can also increase performance for Korean and Japanese, but often shifts the responses toward English, highlighting the importance of multilingual data to preserving target-language responses.

</details>


### [147] [Hierarchical Latent Prediction for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05806)
*Chang Shi,Tim Pearce,Manan Tomar,Siddhartha Sen,John Langford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiLP通过引入辅助的高层级抽象潜变量来减少潜空间扩展中的误差累积，从而改善语言模型在长程推理和规划上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Next-Token Prediction采用教师强制训练范式，在长程推理和规划任务上可能不是最优的。已有的多token预测（MTP）和NextLat等方法虽然试图缓解问题，但要么预测视野有限，要么在潜空间多步展开时存在累积误差。

Method: HiLP引入了辅助的高层级抽象潜变量（hierarchical latent）作为额外的训练目标，通过更高层级的抽象表征来减少潜空间多步预测展开中的误差累积效应。

Result: 实验表明HiLP能够产生更长视野的连贯信念状态表征，在代码生成和多步推理基准上表现有效，并且提升了投机解码（speculative decoding）的效率。

Conclusion: 分层潜变量预测是一种有效缓解潜空间展开误差累积、增强语言模型长程推理能力的方法。

Abstract: While standard Next-Token Prediction (NTP) lays the foundation of language model pre- training, its teacher-forced training paradigm may not be optimal for long-horizon reasoning and planning. Recent works such as Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Next-Latent prediction (NextLat) try to mitigate the problem through predicting multiple future tokens and self-supervised prediction in the latent space. However, those auxiliary objectives either have a limited horizon or suffer from compounding error from multi-step rollout. We introduce Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP), which introduces an auxiliary higher-level abstract latent to help reduce the error accumulation effect in latent-space rollouts. Experiments show that HiLP can lead to longer-horizon coherent belief state representation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across coding and multi-step reasoning benchmarks, and offers more speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [148] [M$^3$R-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Metaphor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05817)
*Hong Jiang,Junnan Zhu,Jingwang Huang,Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Jiang Zhong,Ruirui Chen,Jingman Shi,Hao Wu,Nayu Liu,Xinyi Jiang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M³R-Bench，一个包含1,000个图文对、基于证据的统一隐喻理解基准，提供隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感和分阶段解释的联合标注；同时提出M³R-Reasoner，结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习，仅用8B参数骨干即在四个统一任务指标上超越更大规模专有MLLM，在视觉证据和情感论证得分上分别比GPT-5.5高出28.45和30.11分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐喻理解基准通过孤立子任务进行评估，缺乏基于证据的解释，难以判断模型是否建立了根植于视觉和文本线索的跨模态目标-源映射。

Method: 构建统一且基于证据的基准M³R-Bench（含1,000个图文实例，人工验证标注），基于概念隐喻理论和非字面语言理解理论，提供隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感及分阶段解释的联合标注，遵循"证据识别-映射建立-情感推断"流程。并提出M³R-Reasoner，结合课程式推理监督和任务感知强化学习来对齐模型推理与隐喻解释。

Result: 仅用8B参数骨干的M³R-Reasoner在四项统一任务指标上超越更大规模的专有MLLM；在视觉证据和情感论证得分上比GPT-5.5分别提升28.45和30.11分，在平均评分标准上超越Claude-Sonnet-4.6达8.00分。

Conclusion: 现有多模态大模型常忽视视觉证据、依赖表面文本线索且目标-源映射不准确，存在跨模态证据-映射不匹配问题；M³R-Reasoner通过课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习能有效缓解该问题，以较小模型规模实现更优的隐喻理解性能。

Abstract: Metaphor enables the understanding of abstract concepts through cross-domain mappings while conveying affective attitudes. In multimodal scenarios, visual and textual information jointly construct Target--Source mappings, requiring both conceptual understanding and cross-modal reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate metaphor understanding through isolated subtasks and lack evidence-grounded explanations, making it difficult to assess whether models establish mappings grounded in visual and textual cues.To address these limitations, we introduce M$^3$R-Bench, a unified and evidence-grounded benchmark containing 1,000 image--text instances with human-verified annotations. Guided by Conceptual Metaphor Theory and theories of nonliteral language understanding, M$^3$R-Bench provides joint annotations for metaphor occurrence, Target--Source mapping, sentiment, and stage-wise explanations following ``evidence identification--mapping establishment--sentiment inference.''Evaluations on M$^3$R-Bench reveal that existing models often overlook visual evidence, rely on superficial textual cues, and produce inaccurate Target--Source mappings, exposing a cross-modal evidence--mapping mismatch. To address this mismatch, we propose M$^3$R-Reasoner, which combines curriculum-based reasoning supervision with task-aware reinforcement learning to align model reasoning with metaphor interpretation. Experiments show that, with only an 8B-parameter backbone, M$^3$R-Reasoner outperforms larger proprietary MLLMs across four unified-task metrics and improves Visual Evidence and Sentiment Justification scores over GPT-5.5 by 28.45 and 30.11 points, respectively, while surpassing Claude-Sonnet-4.6 by 8.00 points in mean rubric score. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hongshi4/M3R-Bench.

</details>


### [149] [Decomposed Entailment for Factuality Checking and Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05823)
*Achir Oukelmoun,Nasredine Semmar,Gaël De Chalendar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HallDetect是一个轻量级、无需参考、黑盒的幻觉检测框架，通过将生成内容分解为原子声明，并用紧凑的编码器蕴含模型结合多尺度源块对比与不对称聚合评分来检测幻觉，在三个基准上优于同等资源的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）常因事实不一致（如幻觉）而降低可靠性，现有幻觉检测方法要么依赖生成式模型成本高昂，要么需要参考文档，亟需一个轻量、参考无关且适用于广泛源导向生成任务的高效检测框架。

Method: HallDetect采用基于分解的事实评估思路：将生成内容分解为原子声明（atomic claims），每个声明通过一个紧凑的基于编码器的蕴含模型进行验证；该模型采用对比学习公式，基于多尺度（multi-scale）源块库进行匹配验证；最后通过不对称评分聚合所有声明的验证结果，即单个被高置信度反驳的声明即可判定整个响应存在幻觉，同时生成声明到文本段的审计轨迹用于错误定位。

Result: 在统一的受控协议下（所有方法共享4-bit量化骨干和消费级硬件预算），HallDetect在四个基准中的三个上优于同等资源的生成式与基于嵌入的基线方法，且在跨骨干族（backbone families）时保持稳定性，并提供声明到文本段的精确定位审计轨迹。

Conclusion: HallDetect验证了基于分解的轻量编码器方法在幻觉检测中的有效性：不仅性能优于同等资源的生成式和嵌入基线，还在广泛的源导向生成任务中保持稳健，同时通过审计轨迹支持错误定位，为低成本、可解释的幻觉检测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often compromised by factual inconsistencies, including hallucinations---cases where generated content is not supported by the underlying source. We present HallDetect, a lightweight, reference-free, and black-box framework for hallucination detection that we evaluate not only on summarization but across a broader range of source-grounded generation settings. HallDetect builds on decomposition-based factuality evaluation: generated content is decomposed into atomic claims, each verified by a compact encoder-based entailment model through a contrastive formulation over a multi-scale library of source chunks, and aggregated with an asymmetric score in which a single confidently contradicted claim flags the response. Under a controlled protocol in which all methods share the same 4-bit quantized backbones and consumer-grade hardware budget, HallDetect outperforms comparably resourced generative and embedding-based baselines on three of four benchmarks while remaining stable across backbone families, and yields a claim-to-span audit trail that localizes each error.

</details>


### [150] [MoCA: Implicit Social Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05825)
*Wenhao Xu,Kaiwen Zhang,Hao Li,Maowei You,Yongzheng Ji,Siyuan Zuo,Jingxuan Yu,Sina A,Xinyao Tan,Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式社会情境分析(MoCA)任务，从情感、意图和立场三个维度系统建模隐式社会交流，并构建了包含3,108个多模态实例的高质量基准数据集。作者进一步提出基于冲突驱动的溯因推理框架(CoDAR)，通过建模观察表达与预期真实行为之间的认知冲突来推断隐藏心理状态，实验表明该方法显著提升了多模态大语言模型的性能，但距离人类推理仍有较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社交交流（如情感和意图）往往以高度隐式的方式传达，真实含义通过间接的、基于社会和文化背景的信号表达，而非显式陈述。这类隐式社会情境在实际交互中普遍存在，但缺乏形式化和系统化的研究框架，现有模型也因依赖显式线索而难以处理此类任务。

Method: 首先引入MoCA任务，从情感、意图和立场三个关键维度系统建模隐式社会情境；然后构建包含3,108个来自真实来源的多模态实例的高质量基准数据集，并带有细粒度的认知标注（谁对谁表达了什么，如何及为何传达）；最后提出CoDAR框架，通过建模观察到的表达与预期真实行为之间的差异作为认知冲突，从而推断隐藏的心理状态。

Result: 实验表明最先进的多模态大语言模型在MoCA任务上表现困难，而CoDAR框架能显著提升模型性能，但模型与人类推理能力之间仍存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 隐式社会理解是当前多模态大语言模型面临的根本性难题，MoCA任务和数据集为该领域提供了系统化研究框架，CoDAR展示了通过认知冲突建模提升推理能力的可行性，但模型与人类之间的差距凸显了这一领域的挑战性和未来研究空间。

Abstract: Human social communication, such as affection and intent, is often conveyed in highly implicit ways, where underlying meanings are expressed through indirect, socially and culturally grounded signals rather than explicit statements. Such implicit social contexts are pervasive in real-world interactions, yet there remains a lack of a formal and systematic framework for studying them. In this paper, we introduce Implicit Social Context Analysis (MoCA), a novel task that systematically models implicit social scenarios along three key dimensions: affection, intent, and stance. We construct a high-quality benchmark containing 3,108 multimodal instances collected from real-world sources, with fine-grained cognitive annotations revealing who expresses what toward whom, as well as how and why it is conveyed. Using the MoCA dataset, we show that state-of-the-art multimodal large language models struggle significantly with this task because of their reliance on explicit cues and limited ability to reason over latent social contexts. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning (CoDAR), a novel framework that models the discrepancy between observed expressions and expected truthful behavior as cognitive conflict, thereby enabling the inference of hidden mental states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoDAR substantially improves model performance. Nevertheless, a large gap from human reasoning remains, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of implicit social understanding.

</details>


### [151] [Enhancing Social Intelligence in LLMs with Hierarchical Reasoning and Utterance-Level Goal Rewarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05832)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Kakam Chong,Shuai Xiao,DeXin Kong,Qingyuan Tian,Chen Ju,Xu Yan,Shuai Zhao,Fei Huang,Rui Wang,Shuguang Han,jufeng chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TSR框架（Think-Strategy-Response）和LHRL-VGR算法，将社交对话分解为高层策略规划和低层语言执行两个层次，通过基于目标达成分数方差的动态奖励路由来优化LLM在动态社交交互中的表现，在SOTOPIA基准上以Qwen2.5-7B超越了GPT-4o基线7.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在结构化任务上表现优异，但在动态社交交互中面临挑战——当前方法对所有话语施加统一的基于目标的奖励，忽视了每轮对话目标的特异性以及潜在策略的合理性，缺乏对长期目标协调和快速适应的建模能力。

Method: 受计划行为理论启发，提出TSR框架将社交对话分解为两级层次：高层策略规划与低层语言执行。为优化该框架，引入线性化分层强化学习结合方差门控奖励（LHRL-VGR）算法，根据目标达成分数的方差动态路由奖励，在目标完成与策略遵循之间取得平衡。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上，TSR框架微调的Qwen2.5-7B智能体在目标完成成功率上超越GPT-4o基线7.32%，在多智能体社交协商任务中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过将社交对话分解为策略规划和语言执行两个层次，并结合方差门控的动态奖励机制，可以有效提升LLM在复杂社交交互中的目标达成能力，证明了层次化强化学习在社交智能领域的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel in structured tasks but struggle with dynamic social interactions, where success requires long-term goal coordination and rapid adaptation. Current methods often apply uniform goal-based rewards to every utterance, overlooking the specificity of objectives at each dialogue turn and failing to account for the rationale of potential strategies. Inspired by the Theory of Planned Behavior, we propose the Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) framework, which decomposes social dialogue into two hierarchical stages: high-level strategic planning and low-level linguistic execution. To optimize TSR, we introduce Linearized Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Variance-Gated Rewards (LHRL-VGR), a novel algorithm that dynamically routes rewards - balancing goal completion and strategy adherence - based on the variance of goal achievement scores. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark show that our approach fine-tunes a Qwen2.5-7B agent to surpass the GPT-4o baseline by 7.32% in goal completion success, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in multi-agent social negotiation tasks.

</details>


### [152] [MameLoshnLM: Yiddish Language Model and Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05850)
*Uri Katz,Omer Goldman,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Reut Tsarfaty,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MameLoshnLM，首个开源8B参数专为意第绪语构建的语言模型，通过高质量预训练语料库Oytser和多任务基准Kashes解决意第绪语NLP资源匮乏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意第绪语虽有丰富的文本传统，但数字资源稀缺、可靠评估资源不足，现有多语言语料库和基准包含大量噪声、机器翻译和错误分类文本，不适合作为该语言的代理，阻碍了意第绪语语言建模的发展。

Method: 构建两个核心资源：Oytser（高质量意第绪语预训练语料，融合当代网络来源与文学材料）和Kashes（多任务基准，涵盖翻译、语言分析、信息抽取和语言理解）；基于Llama 3.1 8B继续预训练得到MameLoshnLM。

Result: MameLoshnLM在基准任务上优于同规模开源基线模型，不仅定量指标优于通用多语言模型，还更好地捕获了定义语言的词汇和形态模式，揭示了噪声网页级多语言数据对低资源语言的广泛失败模式。

Conclusion: 为意第绪语NLP提供了基础，并提供了在历史丰富但数字代表性不足的语言中开发语言模型的实用模板。

Abstract: We present MameLoshnLM, the first open-source 8B-parameter language model built specifically for Yiddish. Despite Yiddish's rich textual tradition, its limited digital presence and the scarcity of reliable evaluation resources have constrained progress in Yiddish language modeling. Existing multilingual corpora and benchmarks are often poor proxies for the language, containing substantial amounts of noisy, machine-translated, and misclassified text. We address these gaps by introducing Oytser, a high-quality Yiddish pretraining corpus that combines contemporary web-native sources with literary materials, and Kashes, a multi-task benchmark spanning translation, linguistic analysis, information extraction, and language understanding. Using these resources, we continue pretraining Llama 3.1 8B to obtain MameLoshnLM. Across the tasks in the benchmark, MameLoshnLM outperforms open baselines of similar scale. Our analyses show that these gains are not only quantitative: relative to general-purpose multilingual models, MameLoshnLM better captures language-defining lexical and morphological patterns, pointing to a broader failure mode of noisy web-scale multilingual data for low-resource languages. Our results provide both a foundation for Yiddish NLP and a practical template for language model development in historically rich but digitally underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [153] [Mapping Similarity Spaces across Embedding Models with Synthetic Query Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05857)
*Marcin Rozmus,Peter van der Putten*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Synthetic Query Probing方法，通过从文档生成查询来构建受控的query-chunk对，从而在无需标注参考的情况下学习不同嵌入模型之间相似度分数的映射关系，实现跨模型分数校准和阈值迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖相似度分数检索内容，但不同嵌入模型因几何性质不同导致分数不可直接比较，这阻碍了模型迁移和阈值复用。现有方法缺乏大规模、无参考的跨模型相似度行为分析手段。

Method: 提出Synthetic Query Probing（SQP）框架：从文档中生成查询以创建受控的query-chunk对，利用线性、isotonic和quantile映射学习分数转换函数，在多个嵌入配置上进行评估，实现对跨模型相似度行为的无参考大规模分析。

Result: 在SciFact和专有语料上的实验表明，不同嵌入模型在排序上基本一致但绝对分数存在系统性失真；学习的映射函数能部分对齐分数空间并改善阈值可迁移性，其中isotonic回归效果最佳。

Conclusion: 跨模型校准是必要的，Synthetic Query Probing可作为分析嵌入可比性的可扩展框架，为RAG系统的模型迁移和阈值复用提供支持。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems rely on similarity scores to retrieve relevant content, yet scores are not directly comparable across embedding models due to differing geometric properties, complicating model migration and limiting threshold reuse. We study how similarity scores can be related by learning mappings between score distributions rather than embeddings. We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [154] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiBench——一个基于序列的闭卷自动评分基准，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在抗体表位推理方面的能力。EpiBench包含1,609个基于结构接触、功能B细胞实验和深度突变扫描逃逸数据筛选的样本，覆盖五个相互关联的任务（靶向区域发现、抗体条件性表位识别、表位分箱、功能性表位评估和抗体逃逸评估）。对九个通用LLM的评测显示，当前LLM只能捕获部分表位相关信号，在抗体特异性序列锚定、长上下文残基定位和生物学推理方面仍存在明显局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表位决定抗体与抗原的结合位置并影响下游治疗特性（如功能阻断和逃逸抗性），是抗体药物发现的核心。虽然LLM展现出强大的生物医学推理能力，但现有表位资源要么聚焦于孤立的预测任务，要么依赖专门的结构设定，缺乏一个能系统评估LLM在抗体制药全流程中表位推理能力的基准。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准：(1) 从结构抗体-抗原接触、功能B细胞实验和深度突变扫描逃逸测量中筛选1,609个样本；(2) 设计五个连接任务：靶向区域发现、抗体条件性表位识别、表位分箱、功能性表位评估和抗体逃逸评估；(3) 采用受控采样减少捷径性评估伪影；(4) 使用闭卷、基于序列、自动可评分的形式。

Result: 评估九个通用LLM并通过任务特定基线、抗原长度分层、显式推理对比和失败模式检查进行分析。结果表明当前LLM能捕获部分表位相关信号，但在抗体特异性序列锚定、长上下文残基定位和生物性推理方面仍有限。

Conclusion: EpiBench为测量和改进序列感知生物医学LLM提供了一个诊断性测试平台，推动可靠LLM辅助抗体验证发现的发展。该基准填补了通用蛋白基准不评估跨抗体开发工作流表位中心决策的空白。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [155] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍FormBharo，一个为印度低识字率人群设计的语音代理系统，通过电话对话自动填写结构化表单，将LLM与确定性规则验证相结合，在严格的延迟和成本预算下运行。该系统正在ARMMAN NGO试点用于低收入印地语母亲的妇幼健康计划注册，并发布了包含960个模拟通话、3760轮对话测试的FormVoiceAgentBench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度几乎所有社会福利都始于填表，但最需要这些福利的人往往不识字。面对面服务需要依赖前线的卫生工作者逐一为受益人注册，效率低下且浪费有限的人力资源。需要一个语音对话系统来填补这一鸿沟。

Method: FormBharo将大语言模型与确定性、基于规则的验证及流程控制相结合，在电话通话中填充结构化表单。系统通过基于帕累托的加权和标量化方法，在准确性、成本和延迟三者之间平衡选择可部署的模型配置。同时构建了FormVoiceAgentBench基准，使用人类录制的印地语音频配合3760轮多轮对话测试来评估语音转写、信息提取、回复生成等组件及端到端性能。

Result: 当LLM接收到有真实噪声的语音转写而非参考文本时，表单完成率最多下降约41个百分点。基于规则的控制器能恢复许多轮次级提取错误，使更小、更便宜的模型在表单完成率上达到或超越前沿模型。组件性能无法预测端到端性能：GPT-5.5在参考转写上的轮次级提取准确率达99.8%但表单完成排名较低。没有单一模型能同时在准确性、成本和延迟上最优。

Conclusion: 错误在流水线中既会传播也会抵消，最优模型选择只能通过端到端评估来确定。该系统是首个为该人群试点用于填写注册表单的语音代理，并已开放发布基准供社区进一步研究。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [156] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteerWrite是一个无需训练的框架，用于个性化协同写作，通过消除对梯度更新的依赖，实现基础模型在专业领域的有效适配，并在多个数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽在个性化方面有潜力，但缺乏专业领域知识。传统微调方案计算成本高且难以应对快速数据更新，检索增强生成无法提供细粒度的token级引导，而聊天式接口未充分利用生产力协同写作范式。

Method: SteerWrite采用训练无关（training-free）的方法，不需要梯度更新即可将基础模型适配到专业领域，并针对小数据集进行了特定设计。

Result: 在各种数据集、指标和模型上达到了最先进的性能，显著减少了人工编辑工作量。

Conclusion: SteerWrite框架有效解决了LLM个性化协同写作中的专业领域适配问题，在不进行梯度更新的情况下实现了卓越性能，验证了训练无关方法在个性化写作任务中的可行性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SiPE是一种语法信息的位置嵌入方法，在预训练期间从依存句法树中学习轻量级语法先验，并将其注入到绝对、相对和旋转三种主流位置嵌入族中。在自回归解码器中，该先验与相对位置项乘法耦合效果最佳；在编码器中，直接添加到输入嵌入效果最好。该方法在SyntaxGym基准上提升10.3%，困惑度降低9.0%，GLUE分数提升8.2%，且仅需单一解析即可实现，无需推理时的多解析边际化，建立了语法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer的位置嵌入仅编码标记距离和顺序，却对句法结构不敏感。尽管存在多种语法注入方法，但它们往往在推理时需要边际化多个解析或舍弃语法信息，且大多数方法以牺牲困惑度为代价换取语法能力提升。本文旨在设计一种既能利用句法结构先验、又不损害语言建模质量、同时保持低推理成本的语法注入方法。

Method: SiPE在预训练阶段从依存句法解析中学习轻量级句法先验，并将该先验注入到三种主流位置嵌入族（绝对、相对、旋转）中，适用于编码器和解码器架构，同时不修改自注意力机制及模型其余结构。作者系统研究了先验注入的最佳位置与方式，发现取决于架构：对于使用相对位置嵌入的自回归解码器，先验与注意力分数中的相对位置项进行乘法耦合效果最好；而对于编码器，先验直接添加到输入嵌入中效果最佳，与各编码器原生的位置机制组合使用。

Result: 经预训练的SiPE模型在SyntaxGym基准上最高提升10.3%，同时相比无语法监督的基线模型困惑度降低9.0%（多数现有语法注入方法反而会降低此指标）。此外，SiPE在真实语言理解任务上同样有效，在GLUE基准上最高提升8.2%。与需要推理时边际化多个解析的语法语言模型不同，SiPE只需条件化单一解析，建立了语法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: SiPE验证了句法结构先验可以以轻量级方式融入各种主流位置嵌入体系，且在提升语法泛化能力的同时不损害甚至改善语言建模质量和下游理解能力。该方法在推理时仅需单一解析，避免了现有方法的高推理开销，证明句法监督与推理效率并非不可兼得。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [158] [Decolonizing Linguistic Policies in Automated Speech Recognition: A Framework for Cross-Culturally Competent Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06141)
*Jay L. Cunningham,Mark Atta Mensah,Richard Martinez,Joao Vieira da Silva Neto,Efi Dawodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判性地分析了自动语音识别（ASR）及其语音界面中低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体所遭遇的系统性失败，指出这些失败不仅是技术误差，更是再现殖民语言等级秩序的隐性语言政策，并提出"三重伤害"（3M）分类法、七层情境化模型及参与式审计框架以推动文化胜任的ASR发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR技术的普遍部署正深刻影响公共服务、医疗和教育等关键领域的访问机会，但在低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体上持续存在严重的识别失败。作者认为，这些失败不能仅被理解为技术缺陷，而应被视为隐性的语言政策——它们系统性地排斥某些说话者的声音，再现了殖民语言等级，加剧了语言和社会不平等。

Method: 本文采用跨学科理论框架，综合语言学资本理论、种族语言学意识形态、语言政策研究以及去殖民计算等视角，从数据、指标和模型先验三个维度揭示ASR系统中谁的声音能被机器辨识的机制。在此基础上提出两个核心分析工具：（1）三重伤害（3M）分类法——误识别（Misrecognition）、错位（Misalignment）与不信任（Mistrust）；（2）面向ASR语言多样性的七层情境化模型。最后提出参与式框架和最低审计协议，将受影响社区定位为共同设计者、评估者和治理伙伴。

Result: 论文提出了可操作的分析框架：3M分类法用于系统化识别ASR系统对边缘化语言群体造成的多重伤害；七层情境化模型为全面评估ASR的语言多样性提供了结构化视角；参与式框架与最低审计协议为构建文化胜任的ASR系统提供了实践路径。

Conclusion: 作者主张，解决ASR公平问题需要超越单纯的技术修复，采取去殖民化的研究与实践路径，将受影响社区纳入ASR系统的设计、评估和治理全过程，从而在技术系统中实现真正的语言公正。

Abstract: This paper focuses on automatic speech recognition (ASR) and ASR-mediated voice interfaces that shape access to public services, healthcare, and education. We argue that persistent failures for low-resource, Indigenous, and non-standard language varieties are not only technical errors, but also implicit linguistic policies that reproduce colonial language hierarchies. Drawing on linguistic capital, raciolinguistic ideology, language policy research, and decolonial computing, we show how data, metrics, and model priors determine whose voices become machine-legible. We introduce the Three Harms (3M) taxonomy---Misrecognition, Misalignment, and Mistrust---and a seven-layer situatedness model for linguistic diversity in ASR and ASR-mediated voice interfaces. We then propose a participatory framework and minimum audit protocol for culturally competent ASR, positioning affected communities as co-designers, evaluators, and governance partners.

</details>


### [159] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在VisualWebArena和WebArena上测量了六种观察模式（文本、像素或两者结合）在八个站点-模型组合上的表现，考察按任务动态选择模式的价值。研究发现各模式互补且结果受噪声干扰，尽管理论上最优oracle增益很大，但重复运行同模式的收益与新增模式相近。唯一稳健的是成本约束：仅将无模式能解决的任务交给最廉价模式，可在8/8单元中不损成功率地节省9.5-30.6%成本。五种路由策略均无法稳健优于固定选择单一模式，核心障碍在于路由监督信号受限于代理自身的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过按任务动态选择观察模式（文本、像素或组合）来提升Web代理的性能，而非对所有任务固定使用一种观察模式，以回答"按任务选择模式能带来什么"这一核心问题。

Method: 在VisualWebArena和WebArena上构建8个站点-模型组合单元，测量6种观察模式的成功率；通过oracle分析理论上限，通过重复运行评估run-to-run噪声；设计并测试5种路由策略（模式选择、强模式开支决策、零成本文本规则、置信度级联、成本分层），并进行跨单元相关性与噪声区间分析。

Result: 各观察模式互补性强，彼此可解决对方遗漏的任务且最优选择在不同任务集间反转；最优oracle增益看似很大但被12-14%的run-to-run噪声夸大；成本约束策略在8/8单元中稳定节省9.5-30.6%成本且成功率不变；5种路由策略均无稳健优势，仅在数据最稀疏单元出现脆弱例外；路由监督与代理成功率相关系数达0.95。

Conclusion: 当前代理架构下，路由策略难以稳健超越固定模式选择，其根本限制在于路由监督标签由代理自身成功率决定——代理越弱，可用路由标签越少，这恰是路由最需要的场景。该限制属于当前代理而非路由本身；随着更强大代理出现，标签供应与路由机会同步上升，路由可能变得有价值。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [160] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSy-RAG，一个模块化神经符号RAG框架，从检索到的文本块合成可溯源的Prolog模块，将布尔声明编码为语义谓词，通过联合嵌入进行谓词检索与组合，并引入符号知识缺口检测机制来识别缺失的用户事实并触发后续交互。在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练即达到61.1%准确率，显著优于同模型RAG基线（42.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统存在三个关键问题：(1) 推理过程不透明，中间推理步骤难以验证，无法可靠归属到具体证据；(2) 缺乏对缺失用户特定上下文的系统检测，导致输出不完整或错误。

Method: (1) 从每个检索到的文本块生成语义谓词，编码布尔声明，可依赖用户事实；(2) 使用联合自然语言-代码嵌入进行谓词检索并组合成Prolog查询；(3) 提出符号知识缺口检测机制，识别影响查询结果的缺失用户事实并自动触发后续交互；(4) 执行Prolog查询产生确定性答案和透明执行追踪。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练，NeSy-RAG达到61.1%准确率，优于同模型RAG基线（42.8%），提升了约18.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将RAG与神经符号方法结合，NeSy-RAG实现了可解释、可溯源的推理过程，并能系统性地检测和弥补缺失的用户上下文，显著提升了RAG在复杂问答任务上的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [161] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GB/T-Bench，首个面向国家标准文件结构化审查的基准测试，包含GB/T审查分类法（25种可诊断错误类型）、可控反例生成机制（从488份文档生成7,306个可追溯的审查错误实例），以及一个多智能体框架GB/T-Reviewer。实验显示最强LLM仅达到0.3280 CMCS（专家为0.6640），而GB/T-Reviewer将其提升至0.5094，揭示了规则密集型文档审查中人类与LLM之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注领域知识和问答任务，忽视了专业文档的内在质量审查。国家标准文件（如GB/T）篇幅长、结构高度规范，且受明确的规则约束，但对此类文件的审查严重依赖人类专家，成本高且难以规模化，亟需一个系统化评估LLM在此类规则密集型文档审查能力的基准。

Method: （1）构建GB/T Review Taxonomy，一个涵盖文档结构、范围一致性、规范性语态、术语一致性和规范性引用五个维度、25种可诊断错误类型的层次化分类体系；（2）提出可控反例生成机制，结合确定性规则与受限LLM改写，从488份文档生成7,306个可追溯的审查错误实例；（3）设计面向诊断的评估协议，要求错误位置、审查维度和错误类型三重精确匹配，并引入文档级覆盖率指标；（4）提出GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，将审查知识转化为专门技能，协调全局检查、目标诊断、规则扫描和结果验证四个模块。

Result: 对14个主流LLM的实验结果表明：最强模型仅达到0.3280的CMCS分数，而人类专家为0.6640，存在显著差距；GB/T-Reviewer将最佳CMCS提升至0.5094，验证了结构化技能协调在规则密集型文档审查中的价值。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了国家标准文档内在质量审查基准的空白，证明了结构化多智能体技能协调能显著增强LLM在规则密集型文档审查中的表现，为标准制定及其他高风险文档领域的可信AI应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [162] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM评判者的参考无关框架，用于评估面向任务的对话代理基准的质量，包括一致性、复杂性和策略覆盖率三个维度，并提供可操作的诊断信息。该框架在多种领域和评判模型上验证了有效性，适用于合成和人工整理的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向任务的对话代理通常使用策划或自动生成的基准进行评估，但基准本身的质量很少被评估。质量差的基准可能包含不一致的任务、过于简单的场景或有限的策略覆盖，导致不可靠的评估结果。因此亟需一种能够系统评估基准质量的方法。

Method: 提出一个参考无关的框架，利用LLM评判者从三个维度评估基准质量：一致性（consistency）、复杂性（complexity）和策略覆盖率（policy coverage）。同时提供可操作的弱点诊断。通过与独立人类标注的一致性验证框架有效性，并使用不同能力的LLM生成的基准以及进行受控质量退化扰动后的基准进行评估对比。

Result: 跨领域和不同评判模型，所提出的指标能够一致地区分基准质量水平。框架还进一步展示了对人工策划基准的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估合成和人工整理的对话代理基准提供了一种实用的方法，能够识别基准中的薄弱环节并指导基准的改进。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RP-OPSD（Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation），一种针对多语言推理迁移的推理枢轴引导的在线策略自蒸馏方法。该方法通过匹配教师视图（有无英文参考解）之间的分布偏移作为操作代理，将特权蒸馏集中在推理枢轴（即推进或改变推理过程的决策）上，并在17种语言的数学推理基准上取得了优于强基线及OPSD变体的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言推理迁移对扩展LLM推理能力至关重要，但现有OPSD及其变体虽然提供密集的token级监督，却未显式优先处理对跨语言迁移最关键的推理信号。作者将目标语言推理分解为表面文本生成和推理枢轴两部分，认为后者才是决定推理进程的关键，因此需要将特权蒸馏集中在推理枢轴周围。

Method: RP-OPSD通过计算教师视图（有/无英文参考解配对）之间的分布偏移作为操作代理，识别学生生成的rollout中的推理枢轴token，并据此引导特权蒸馏和参考锚定。具体而言，该方法在推理枢轴token上加强特权蒸馏监督，同时降低对仅用于表面实现的token的权重。

Result: 在覆盖17种语言和多个难度级别的数学推理基准上，RP-OPSD优于强多语言推理基线和OPSD变体。进一步分析表明，该方法将特权蒸馏集中在推理控制和问题条件化的状态更新token上，同时降低主要用于表面实现的token的权重。

Conclusion: RP-OPSD通过推理枢轴引导的策略自蒸馏，有效地改善了多语言推理迁移性能，验证了将特权监督集中于推理关键决策点这一思路的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [164] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了程序化工具调用（PTC，将工具暴露为类型化的Python桩并通过代码调用）与原生的JSON工具调用范式。在BFCL v4基准上评估了14个语言模型，发现PTC在11/14模型中匹配或超越JSON调用，在GPT-5.6系列上提升10.6%；在并行扇出场景下13/14匹配或超越，且在上下文旋转（context rot）条件下保持稳定而基线平均下降2.3%。结论是程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用使LLM成为能超越训练数据行动的智能体，对代码能力强的模型而言，程序化工具调用通过脚本替代僵化的JSON调用，能自然实现链式调用和并行化。然而，此前缺乏在成熟基准上对跨代模型进行程序化工具调用的系统性实证评估，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在BFCL v4基准上，对14个语言模型（涵盖当前及先前模型世代）进行实证对比。实验对比两种范式：程序化工具调用（PTC）——将工具暴露为类型化的Python桩，模型通过代码调用，执行与结果返回在单个智能体回合内完成；以及原生JSON工具调用作为基线。评估维度包括整体性能、并行扇出（parallel fan-out）场景以及上下文旋转（context rot）鲁棒性。

Result: 程序化工具调用在BFCL v4上14个模型中有11个匹配或超越原生JSON工具调用；GPT-5.6系列在PTC下比JSON基线提升10.6%。在并行扇出条件下，13/14模型匹配或超越基线。在上下文旋转条件下，PTC保持稳定，而JSON基线平均退化2.3%。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案，其性能随模型发布世代的能力提升而同步提升，展现出与模型能力演进的强相关性。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [165] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将语言模型对外部信号的鲁棒性问题重新定义为"选择性信任"问题，引入MIST基准（四种匹配条件下的推理任务）和SC2W指标（衡量误导信号翻转正确回答的频率），并提出SCOPE方法（基于DPO优化所有四种条件的配对偏好），大幅降低SC2W同时保持对有效上下文的利用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地依赖外部信号进行回答，但一个误导性信号就能将正确回答变为错误。传统上"训练模型抵抗误导信号"的做法存在严重缺陷：一个忽略所有上下文的模型看似鲁棒，却无法利用值得信任的上下文，实际无用。因此需要将问题重新定义为选择性信任——模型应能抵制误导信号，同时仍能信任有价值的上下文。

Method: 1) 提出MIST基准：人类标注的推理基准，每个推理题目在四种匹配条件下呈现（clean、misleading、correct-context、irrelevant-context）；2) 提出SC2W配对指标：统计误导信号将clean-correct回答翻转为错误回答的频率；3) 提出SCOPE方法：挖掘clean-correct/misleading-wrong的失败样本，并在所有四种条件均衡配对的偏好对上优化标准DPO目标，而非仅对误导样本优化。

Result: 在全面的基准研究中，发现模型对误导信号的易感性是普遍存在的。SCOPE方法在流行的开源模型上大幅降低了SC2W指标，同时保持了在上下文为干净、正确或无关时模型利用有效信息的能力。

Conclusion: 模型应基于选择性信任来评估，而非仅基于鲁棒性。研究证明了通过平衡所有条件下的偏好优化，可以在提升抗误导能力的同时保持对有效上下文的正确利用。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>


### [166] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MACRO框架，将Transformer层的动态路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，在不修改模型权重的情况下学习任务特定的层执行路径（包括跳过、重复和残差加法），通过top-k Viterbi算法解码高概率候选程序，在多个推理和知识基准上平均提升5.0%准确率，比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%，同时将路由搜索时间缩短9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM按顺序逐层执行，而动态层路由（如层重复、跳过等）可以提升性能。但现有路由方法存在三大痛点：需要更新模型权重、每测试实例需要昂贵的搜索循环、或推理时需要ground-truth标签。作者旨在提出一种无需修改参数、无需昂贵搜索且不依赖标签的轻量级动态路由框架。

Method: MACRO将层路由建模为依赖于层索引、计算预算阶段、方向位移和操作符上下文的条件马尔可夫策略，支持跳过、重复和残差隐藏状态加三种操作。路由分布通过训练数据上的反馈信号进行更新，并使用top-k Viterbi算法解码出高概率的候选执行程序，最终选择最优路由路径。

Result: 在多个开放权重LLM的推理和知识基准上，MACRO相比未路由基线平均提升5.0%准确率，小模型上收益最大；相比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%准确率，并将路由搜索时间从14.8小时降至1.6小时（9.4倍加速）。

Conclusion: MACRO证明了在不修改LLM权重的前提下，通过上下文相关的马尔可夫策略学习任务特定的层路由路径，能够显著提升推理和知识任务性能，同时大幅降低路由搜索开销，为动态层路由提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [VLAff: Vision-Language-Affordance Model for Unified Actionable Affordances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05215)
*Jihoon Oh,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用人类第一视角视频学习物体中心可行动作affordance的框架，构建了包含204K episodes的大规模数据集EgoAffordance，并在此基础上训练了统一的视觉-语言-动作基础模型VLAff，可同时预测视觉affordance热图、抓取姿态和运动轨迹，并在真实机器人上实现零样本操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类视频学习操作技能对可扩展的机器人学习很有前景，但人类与机器人之间的具身（embodiment）差异使得这一任务极具挑战。已有方法难以跨越这种差异，因此需要提取与具身无关的、以物体为中心的可用性（affordance）信息来弥合人类与机器人之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 利用最先进的3D运动恢复结构（SfM）和手部网格重建从第一视角人类视频中提取三类可行动affordance：视觉affordance（哪里交互）、抓取affordance（如何抓取）、轨迹affordance（如何移动）。2) 构建EgoAffordance大规模数据集（204K episodes，含5.6M视觉affordance和11.6M抓取/轨迹affordance）。3) 提出VLAff，一个基于大型视觉-语言模型（VLM）的统一基础模型，学习所有可行动affordance之间的跨模态关联，根据视觉观察和指令生成视觉affordance热图、抓取姿态和轨迹，并通过3D场景信息转换为可直接执行的机器人动作。

Result: VLAff在视觉affordance预测任务上达到最先进水平（SOTA），并成功应用于真实机器人场景，包括零样本操作和affordance引导的机器人学习，展示了良好的跨具身泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过以物体为中心的可行动affordance作为人类到机器人知识迁移的中间表征，结合大规模数据和大语言模型的统一框架，可以有效克服具身差异问题，实现从人类视频到真实机器人操作的泛化，为可扩展的机器人操作学习提供了一条具有前景的路径。

Abstract: Learning manipulation skills from human videos is promising for scalable robot learning. However, the embodiment mismatch between humans and robots makes this challenging. One promising solution is to learn object-centric actionable affordances that are embodiment-agnostic. In this work, we propose a framework that leverages egocentric human videos with state-of-the-art 3D Structure-from-Motion and hand mesh reconstruction to extract actionable affordances such as visual, grasp, and trajectory affordances that explicitly encode where to interact, how to grasp, and how to move. We construct EgoAffordance, a large-scale dataset comprising 204K episodes with 5.6M visual affordances and 11.6M grasp and trajectory affordances. Building on this, we introduce VLAff, a large vision-language model-based unified foundation model that learns cross-modal correlations across all actionable affordances. Given a visual observation and instruction, VLAff generates visual affordance heatmaps, grasp poses, and trajectories, which are then converted into directly executable actions by utilizing 3D scene information. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that VLAff not only achieves state-of-the-art performance on visual affordance prediction, but can also be effectively applied to real robot applications such as zero-shot manipulation and affordance-guided robot learning.

</details>


### [168] [A System for Train Condition Monitoring and Structural Health Assessment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05221)
*Maximilian Posner,Martin Dazer,Daniela Lauer,Robert Winkler-Höhn,Mathilde Laporte,Tobias Herrmann,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将结构传感器技术与AI数据分析相结合的新型实时车辆状态监测与碰撞检测方法，旨在解决干线铁路向GoA4全自动运营过渡中的安全监测难题。该方法涵盖碰撞/损伤检测、基于状态的维护和长期数据分析三大应用，结果显示其具有可行性，可提升运营安全并支持预测性维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁路系统数字化和AI应用的推进正在改变轨道车辆的设计、运营和维护方式，但干线铁路的全自动运营（GoA4）仍受限。主要原因是严格的安规要求和开放运行环境的复杂性，以及现有基于相机、雷达和激光雷达的感知系统虽能检测物体，却难以可靠识别撞击、碰撞和碾轧事件，缺乏有效的实时车辆冲击监测手段。

Method: 提出一个集成结构传感器技术与AI数据分析的实时车辆状态监测与碰撞检测框架。该框架融合了结构传感器（用于感知物理冲击信号）与AI算法（用于数据分析与模式识别），针对三个关键应用场景：自动检测碰撞/结构损伤/碾轧事件、基于持续监测的状态维护、以及支撑车辆设计优化的长期数据分析。

Result: 实验结果显示该方法的可行性，证明了所提框架在实时碰撞检测、结构损伤识别和碾轧事件监测方面的有效性，并展现了其提升运营安全、支持预测性维护策略的能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为干线铁路向GoA4全自动运营过渡提供了可行的安全监测解决方案，通过结构传感器与AI的结合弥补了现有感知系统的不足，有望推动干线铁路全自动化的实现。

Abstract: The ongoing digitalization of rail systems and the increasing use of artificial intelligence (AI) are fundamentally transforming the design, operation, and maintenance of rail vehicles. While fully automated operation at Grade of Automation 4 (GoA4) is well established in metro systems, its deployment in mainline rail remains limited. This is primarily due to stringent safety requirements and the complexity of open operational environments. Current perception systems based on cameras, radar, and lidar are effective in detecting objects but provide limited capability for reliably identifying impacts, collisions, and driving-over events. This paper presents a novel approach for real-time vehicle condition monitoring and impact detection that integrates structural sensor technologies with AI-based data analysis. The proposed framework addresses three key applications: (1) automated detection of impacts, structural damage, and driving-over events, (2) condition-based maintenance enabled by continuous monitoring, and (3) long-term data analytics to support vehicle design optimization. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and highlight its potential to enhance operational safety, enable predictive maintenance strategies, and support the transition toward fully automated operation in mainline rail systems

</details>


### [169] [Failing Gracefully: Mitigating Impact of Inevitable Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05313)
*Duc M. Nguyen,Saad A. Ghani,Andrew Marshall,Allison Andreyev,Gregory J. Stein,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的机器人安全公式，用于评估失败时机器人与周围实体的交互概率及后果严重性，并配套提出FailBench仿真框架（基于MuJoCo）以系统评估各种失效模式下的机器人-环境交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人在家庭环境中与人类、宠物和日常物品共享空间，极易遭遇软件崩溃、硬件退化或不可预测交互等故障。虽然机器人学家尽力减少故障，但一些故障不可避免，因此必须减轻其潜在后果以确保安全可靠的部署。

Method: 提出一种新的安全公式，量化评估失败期间机器人与周围实体之间高影响交互的概率及其后果严重性；同时构建FailBench，一个基于MuJoCo的仿真框架，用于在不同失效模式（如传感问题和执行器故障）下研究机器人-环境交互。

Result: 该方法使机器人能够做出在安全性与任务效率之间取得平衡的知情规划决策，FailBench为系统评估不同失效模式提供了仿真支持。

Conclusion: 安全公式与FailBench共同为在真实家庭环境中开发更安全、更稳健的运动规划和习得策略奠定了基础。

Abstract: Service robots operate in household environments shared with humans, pets, and everyday objects, where they are highly susceptible to failures such as software crashes, hardware degradation, or unpredictable interactions. While roboticists strive to minimize failures, some remain inevitable, making it critical to mitigate their potential consequences for safe and reliable deployment. This paper introduces a novel safety formulation that evaluates both the probability of impactful interactions between robots and surrounding entities during failures, and the severity of their outcomes. By quantifying the impact of failures on different entities, our approach enables robots to make informed planning decisions that balance safety with task efficiency. To support systematic evaluation, we also present FailBench, a MuJoCo-based simulation framework for studying robot-environment interactions under diverse failure modes, including sensing issues and actuator malfunctions. Together, our safety formulation and FailBench provide a foundation for developing safer and more robust motion plans and learned policies in real-world household environments.

</details>


### [170] [Unified Planning-Learning Framework for Robust UUV Navigation Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05365)
*Md Ether Deowan,Eleni Kelasidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于观测的自主导航框架，用于动态水下环境中的无人水下航行器（UUV），结合持久占用地图构建、全局无碰撞规划和基于强化学习的局部风险感知控制，并通过行为树蒸馏与不确定性校准机制提升安全性与训练稳定性，在NVIDIA Isaac Sim高保真仿真中验证了其相对于基准方法的鲁棒性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UUV在动态水下环境中导航面临部分可观测性、传感器噪声和动态障碍物等挑战，现有方法缺乏将全局结构规划与局部反应式控制统一结合的框架，且训练稳定性与安全性难以保证。

Method: 提出观测only的自主导航管线：(1) 基于声纳与深度图像构建持久占用地图；(2) 引入全局清理约束规划器提供长时域结构；(3) 使用强化学习策略处理短距离跟踪与反应式避障；(4) 学习紧凑潜在状态表示编码环境结构、障碍动力学与不确定性；(5) 引入行为树蒸馏与分阶段监督提升安全性和训练稳定性；(6) 通过不确定性校准蒸馏机制，利用在线潜在模型不确定性对教师指导加权，强调学习中的不确定区域；时间到碰撞和清理提示显式保留在规划与局部策略特征中。

Result: 在高保真GPU加速仿真（NVIDIA Isaac Sim）中建立可复现的多随机种子评估协议，结果表明该方法相比仅行为树和标准RL基线在动态条件下具有更高的鲁棒性与安全性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种统一的混合规划-学习架构和可复现的评估方法，为部分可观测条件下的鲁棒UUV自主导航提供了一种通用解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an observation-only autonomy framework for Unmanned Underwater Vehicles (UUVs) navigation in dynamic underwater environments that integrates persistent occupancy mapping, global clearance-aware planning, and risk-aware local control. The proposed pipeline constructs occupancy maps solely from onboard sonar and depth image observations, adapts a clearance-constrained global planner (GP) to provide long-horizon structure, and integrates a reinforcement learning (RL) policy to handle short-range tracking and reactive avoidance. To further support decision-making under partial observability, the system learns a compact latent state representation from onboard sensor data, encoding environmental structure, obstacle dynamics, and uncertainty. Behavior tree (BT) distillation with staged supervision is introduced to improve safety and training stability, while an uncertainty-calibrated distillation mechanism reweights teacher guidance using online latent-model uncertainty, emphasizing uncertain regimes during learning, with time-to-collision (TTC) and clearance cues remaining explicit in planning and local policy features. To demonstrate the efficacy of the framework, a reproducible multi-seed evaluation protocol is established in high-fidelity GPU-accelerated simulation using NVIDIA Isaac Sim, and performance is benchmarked against BT-only and standard RL baselines. The results obtained demonstrate improved robustness and safety under dynamic conditions, thus providing a general pipeline with a unified hybrid planning learning architecture and a reproducible methodology for robust UUV autonomy under partial observability.
